Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the supersymmetry framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 651. The resulting operator achieves bound O(732/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to supersymmetry structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 651/732 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on supersymmetry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 792. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 658 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on supersymmetry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 792. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 658. QED.
